@@ -0,0 +1,58 @@
+import SQLeanite.SQLite.CreateCatalog
+
+namespace SQLeanite.SQLite.AlterCatalog
+open CreateCatalog
+
+def tableConstraint (span : Span) : Bool :=
+  ["CONSTRAINT", "PRIMARY", "UNIQUE", "CHECK", "FOREIGN"].any (keyword · span)
+
+def addOffset : Nat → List Span → Option Nat
+  | _, [] => none
+  | depth, first :: rest =>
+      if first.token == .symbol "(" then addOffset (depth+1) rest
+      else if first.token == .symbol ")" then
+        if depth == 1 then some first.start else addOffset (depth-1) rest
+      else if depth == 1 && first.token == .symbol "," && (rest.head?.any tableConstraint) then
+        some first.start
+      else addOffset depth rest
+
+/-- A rewritten trailing line comment may consume the original ')' token. -/
+def outerEnd : Nat → List Span → Option Nat
+  | _, [] => none
+  | depth, first :: rest =>
+      if first.token == .symbol "(" then outerEnd (depth+1) rest
+      else if first.token == .symbol ")" then
+        if depth == 1 then some first.finish else outerEnd (depth-1) rest
+      else outerEnd depth rest
+
+def columnStart (tokens : List Span) : Option Span := do
+  let rest ← afterKeyword "ALTER" tokens >>= afterKeyword "TABLE"
+  let rest ← match rest with
+    | _ :: dot :: _ :: tail => if dot.token == .symbol "." then some tail else some rest.tail
+    | _ :: tail => some tail
+    | [] => none
+  let rest ← afterKeyword "ADD" rest
+  let rest := (afterKeyword "COLUMN" rest).getD rest
+  rest.head?
+
+def trimEndChar (char : Char) : Bool :=
+  char == ';' || char == ' ' || (char.toNat >= 9 && char.toNat <= 13)
+
+def addColumn (catalog source : String) : Option String := do
+  let chars := catalog.toList
+  let catalogTokens ← (spans (chars.length+1) 0 chars []).toOption
+  let offset ← addOffset 0 catalogTokens
+  let input := source.toList
+  let tokens ← (spans (input.length+1) 0 input []).toOption
+  let start ← columnStart tokens
+  let terminator := tokens.find? (fun span => span.token == .symbol ";")
+  let finish := (terminator.map (·.finish)).getD input.length
+  let definition := input.drop start.start |>.take (finish-start.start)
+  let definition := definition.reverse.dropWhile trimEndChar |>.reverse
+  let rewritten := String.ofList (chars.take offset) ++ ", " ++ String.ofList definition ++
+    String.ofList (chars.drop offset)
+  let tokens ← (spans (rewritten.toList.length+1) 0 rewritten.toList []).toOption
+  discard <| outerEnd 0 tokens
+  return rewritten
+
+end SQLeanite.SQLite.AlterCatalog

+  for batch in [sqliteAlterCatalogCases0, sqliteAlterCatalogCases160, sqliteAlterCatalogCases320, sqliteAlterCatalogCases480, sqliteAlterCatalogCases640, sqliteAlterCatalogCases800, sqliteAlterCatalogCases960, sqliteAlterCatalogCases1120, sqliteAlterCatalogCases1280, sqliteAlterCatalogCases1440, sqliteAlterCatalogCases1600, sqliteAlterCatalogCases1760, sqliteAlterCatalogCases1920] do

+private def expectSQLiteAlterSource : IO Unit := do
+  for (catalog, source, expected) in sqliteAlterSourceCases do
+    let (before, _) ← match CoreSourceSQL.execute Core.State.empty catalog with
+      | .ok result => pure result | .error message => fail message
+    match CoreSourceSQL.execute before source with
+    | .error message =>
+        unless expected == "none" && message == "error in table t after add column: incomplete input" do
+          fail s!"ALTER ADD source error differs: {source}: {message}"
+    | .ok (after, _) =>
+        let some table := after.database.findTable? "t" | fail "altered table is missing"
+        unless SQLite.Text.hexBytes table.catalogSQL.toUTF8.data == expected do
+          fail s!"ALTER ADD source catalog differs: {source}"
+        let some image := CoreFile.encode after.database | fail "altered catalog encode failed"
+        let some reopened := CoreDecode.decode image.fileBytes | fail "altered catalog reopen failed"
+        unless reopened.Equiv after.database do fail "altered catalog round trip differs"

+  ("CREATE TRIGGER truncated_5 AFTER UPDATE ON t BEGIN DELETE FROM t;", "incomplete input"),

+  ("CREATE TRIGGER truncated_10 AFTER UPDATE ON t BEGIN SELECT 1; unexpected token", "near \"unexpected\": syntax error"),
+  ("CREATE TRIGGER truncated_11 AFTER UPDATE ON t BEGIN DELETE FROM t;", "incomplete input")]

+private def expectSQLiteTriggerEof : IO Unit := do
+  for (source, expected) in sqliteTriggerEofCases do
+    match parse source with
+    | .ok _ => fail "incomplete trigger body unexpectedly parsed"
+    | .error message => unless message == expected do
+        fail s!"trigger body diagnostic differs: {message}"

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""ALTER ADD COLUMN: source rewrite, results/errors, storage and cross-engine use.
+
+Preselected rowid-table definitions and ALTER traces include quoted Unicode
+identifiers, table-level primary keys, expression defaults, comments, rollback
+and trailing-line-comment failures. No outcome is filtered. Seeds generate
+new names/values/comments within reused templates; this is not all SQL syntax.
+"""
+import argparse,hashlib,importlib.util,json,os,random,shutil,subprocess,tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('position',ROOT/'scripts/run-json-position-differential.py');helpers=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(helpers)
+def main():
+ p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__);p.add_argument('--seed',type=int,required=True);p.add_argument('--count',type=int,default=128);p.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True);a=p.parse_args();rng=random.Random(a.seed);sqlite=os.environ.get('SQLITE3_BIN','sqlite3');binary=ROOT/'.lake/build/bin/sqleanite';source=helpers.digest();exe=hashlib.sha256(binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+ def run(cmd,sql):
+  q=subprocess.run(cmd,input=sql.encode(),capture_output=True,timeout=120);return dict(code=q.returncode,stdout=q.stdout.decode('latin1'),stderr=q.stderr.decode('latin1'))
+ def csql(path,sql):return run([sqlite,'-batch','-bail',str(path)],sql)
+ def quote(s):return '"'+s.replace('"','""')+'"'
+ def lexquote(s,style):return '['+s+']' if style=='[' else style+s.replace(style,style+style)+style
+ def name():return ''.join(rng.choice('abAZ_019 é中";\'') for _ in range(rng.randrange(1,25)))
+ types=['','TEXT','INTEGER','REAL','NUMERIC','BLOB','VARCHAR(19)','DOUBLE PRECISION'];inputs=[]
+ for i in range(a.count):
+  table='t_'+name();column='added_'+name();t=quote(table);tn=lexquote(table,rng.choice(['"',"'",'`','[']));cn=lexquote(column,rng.choice(['"',"'",'`','[']));c=quote(column)
+  oldcomment=' /* original '+str(rng.randrange(10000000))+'; */ '
+  body=rng.choice(['(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v TEXT)', '(id INTEGER, v TEXT'+oldcomment+', PRIMARY KEY(id))','(id INTEGER, v TEXT, CONSTRAINT pk PRIMARY KEY(id))',"(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, v TEXT DEFAULT ('a;''b')"+oldcomment+')'])
+  create='CREATE TABLE '+tn+oldcomment+body+';';initial=rng.randrange(1,99999);populate="INSERT INTO "+t+" VALUES(1,'old "+str(initial)+"');" if i%3 else ''
+  decl=rng.choice(types);default=rng.choice(["'value "+str(rng.randrange(100000))+"'",'NULL',str(-rng.randrange(1,9999)),"X'0080FF'",'2.25']);definition=cn+' '+decl+' /* new '+str(rng.randrange(1000000))+' */ DEFAULT ('+default+')'
+  ending=rng.choice([';',' /* ending */;',' -- final;\n;',' \t\n;'])
+  alter=rng.choice(['ALTER TABLE ','alter /**/ table '])+tn+rng.choice([' ADD ',' ADD COLUMN '])+definition+ending
+  scenario=i%5
+  if scenario==0:sql=alter+'\nSELECT count(*) FROM '+t+';'
+  elif scenario==1:sql='BEGIN;\n'+alter+'\nCOMMIT;\nSELECT count(*) FROM '+t+';'
+  elif scenario==2:sql='BEGIN;\n'+alter+'\nROLLBACK;\n'+alter
+  elif scenario==3:sql='SELECT count(*) FROM '+t+';\nSAVEPOINT s;\n'+alter+'\nROLLBACK TO s;\nRELEASE s;\n'+alter
+  else:sql=alter+'\nUPDATE '+t+" SET v='changed';\n"
+  # Universal observations remain valid whether ADD succeeds or errors.
+  read='PRAGMA table_info('+t+');SELECT * FROM '+t+' ORDER BY id;'
+  observe='PRAGMA integrity_check;SELECT type,hex(name),hex(tbl_name),hex(sql) FROM sqlite_schema ORDER BY type,name;'+read
+  follow='INSERT INTO '+t+'(id,v) VALUES(99,\'follow\');PRAGMA integrity_check;'+read
+  inputs.append(dict(table=table,column=column,create=create,populate=populate,sql=sql,scenario=scenario,read=read,observe=observe,follow=follow,page_size=512<<(i%8)))
+ cases=[]
+ with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='sqleanite-alter-catalog-') as tmp:
+  folder=Path(tmp)
+  for i,entry in enumerate(inputs):
+   base=folder/(str(i)+'-base.db');made=csql(base,'PRAGMA page_size='+str(entry['page_size'])+';'+entry['create']+entry['populate']);assert made['code']==0,made
+   for mode in ['--verified-file','--verified-wal-file']:
+    left=folder/(str(i)+mode+'-lean.db');right=folder/(str(i)+mode+'-sqlite.db');shutil.copyfile(base,left);shutil.copyfile(base,right)
+    l,c=run([str(binary),mode,str(left)],entry['sql']),csql(right,entry['sql'])
+    lo,co=csql(left,entry['observe']),csql(right,entry['observe']);lr,cr=run([str(binary),mode,str(left)],entry['read']),csql(right,entry['read']);lw,cw=csql(left,entry['follow']),csql(right,entry['follow'])
+    matched=helpers.same(l,c) and lo['code']==co['code']==0 and lo['stdout']==co['stdout'] and lo['stderr']==co['stderr']=='' and lo['stdout'].startswith('ok\n') and helpers.same(lr,cr) and lw['code']==cw['code']==0 and lw['stdout']==cw['stdout'] and lw['stderr']==cw['stderr']==''
+    cases.append(dict(input=i,mode=mode,lean=l,sqlite=c,observation_lean=lo,observation_sqlite=co,reopen_lean=lr,reopen_sqlite=cr,follow_lean=lw,follow_sqlite=cw,matched=matched))
+ assert source==helpers.digest() and exe==hashlib.sha256(binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+ report=dict(seed=a.seed,inputs=inputs,cases=cases,failures=sum(not c['matched'] for c in cases),implementation_sha256=source,executable_sha256=exe,harness_sha256=hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest(),helper_sha256=hashlib.sha256((ROOT/'scripts/run-json-position-differential.py').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),comparison=__doc__.strip(),sqlite_version=subprocess.check_output([sqlite,'--version'],text=True).strip());a.output.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2)+'\n');print(json.dumps(dict(cases=len(cases),failures=report['failures'])));return bool(report['failures'])
+if __name__=='__main__':raise SystemExit(main())

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""A small, explicit compatibility-gap survey, not a global coverage percentage.
+
+Each named feature has one deterministic SQL probe on seeded C-created data.
+Both public file modes compare query results and normalized SQL errors plus
+complete C-observed catalog text, stored base rows and integrity. All probes,
+including failures, are retained. Parser, execution and file failures are not
+silently accepted or filtered. A pass covers only that probe, not its feature.
+"""
+import argparse,hashlib,importlib.util,json,os,random,shutil,subprocess,tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('position',ROOT/'scripts/run-json-position-differential.py');helpers=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(helpers)
+def main():
+ p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__);p.add_argument('--seed',type=int,required=True);p.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True);a=p.parse_args();rng=random.Random(a.seed);sqlite=os.environ.get('SQLITE3_BIN','sqlite3');binary=ROOT/'.lake/build/bin/sqleanite';source=helpers.digest();exe=hashlib.sha256(binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+ def run(cmd,sql):
+  try:
+   q=subprocess.run(cmd,input=sql.encode(),capture_output=True,timeout=10);return dict(code=q.returncode,stdout=q.stdout.decode('latin1'),stderr=q.stderr.decode('latin1'))
+  except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as expired:
+   return dict(code=124,stdout=(expired.stdout or b'').decode('latin1'),stderr=(expired.stderr or b'').decode('latin1'),timed_out=True,timeout_seconds=10)
+ def csql(path,sql):return run([sqlite,'-batch','-bail',str(path)],sql)
+ probes=[
+ ('cte','WITH c AS (SELECT n FROM t) SELECT sum(n) FROM c;'),
+ ('recursive-cte','WITH RECURSIVE c(x) AS (VALUES(1) UNION ALL SELECT x+1 FROM c WHERE x<8) SELECT sum(x) FROM c;'),
+ ('scalar-subquery','SELECT id,(SELECT max(n) FROM t) FROM t ORDER BY id;'),
+ ('correlated-subquery','SELECT a.id,(SELECT sum(b.n) FROM t b WHERE b.id<=a.id) FROM t a ORDER BY a.id;'),
+ ('from-subquery','SELECT sum(n) FROM (SELECT n FROM t WHERE id>1);'),
+ ('view','CREATE VIEW visible AS SELECT id,n FROM t;\nSELECT * FROM visible ORDER BY id;'),
+ ('trigger','CREATE TRIGGER update_note AFTER UPDATE ON t BEGIN UPDATE t SET v=\'triggered\' WHERE id=new.id; END;\nUPDATE t SET n=n+1 WHERE id=1;\nSELECT v FROM t WHERE id=1;'),
+ ('window-row-number','SELECT id,row_number() OVER (ORDER BY n,id) FROM t ORDER BY id;'),
+ ('window-aggregate','SELECT id,sum(n) OVER (ORDER BY id ROWS BETWEEN 1 PRECEDING AND CURRENT ROW) FROM t ORDER BY id;'),
+ ('strict-table','CREATE TABLE strict_tab(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v TEXT) STRICT;\nINSERT INTO strict_tab VALUES(1,23);\nSELECT typeof(v),v FROM strict_tab;'),
+ ('without-rowid','CREATE TABLE no_rowid(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,v INTEGER) WITHOUT ROWID;\nINSERT INTO no_rowid VALUES(\'a\',2);\nSELECT * FROM no_rowid;'),
+ ('composite-primary-key','CREATE TABLE composite(a INTEGER,b INTEGER,PRIMARY KEY(a,b));\nINSERT INTO composite VALUES(1,2);\nSELECT * FROM composite;'),
+ ('unique-constraint','CREATE TABLE uq(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v TEXT UNIQUE);\nINSERT INTO uq VALUES(1,\'same\');\nINSERT INTO uq VALUES(2,\'same\');'),
+ ('check-constraint','CREATE TABLE ck(v INTEGER CHECK(v>0));\nINSERT INTO ck VALUES(-1);'),
+ ('foreign-key','PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;\nCREATE TABLE fk(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,parent INTEGER REFERENCES t(id));\nINSERT INTO fk VALUES(1,999);'),
+ ('generated-column','CREATE TABLE gen(a INTEGER,b INTEGER GENERATED ALWAYS AS (a+1) STORED);\nINSERT INTO gen(a) VALUES(3);\nSELECT * FROM gen;'),
+ ('declared-nocase','CREATE TABLE coll(v TEXT COLLATE NOCASE);\nINSERT INTO coll VALUES(\'a\'),(\'A\');\nSELECT count(*) FROM coll WHERE v=\'a\';'),
+ ('named-index-catalog','CREATE INDEX ix ON t(v);\nSELECT id FROM t ORDER BY v,id;'),
+ ('unique-index-catalog','CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ui ON t(n);\nSELECT n FROM t ORDER BY n;'),
+ ('partial-index','CREATE INDEX pi ON t(n) WHERE n>0;\nSELECT n FROM t WHERE n>0 ORDER BY n;'),
+ ('expression-index','CREATE INDEX ei ON t(lower(v));\nSELECT id FROM t WHERE lower(v)=\'missing\';'),
+ ('alter-add','ALTER TABLE t ADD added TEXT DEFAULT \'new\';\nSELECT added FROM t ORDER BY id;'),
+ ('alter-add-line-comment','ALTER TABLE t ADD added TEXT -- retained line comment\n;'),
+ ('alter-rename-table','ALTER TABLE t RENAME TO renamed;\nSELECT * FROM renamed ORDER BY id;'),
+ ('alter-rename-column','ALTER TABLE t RENAME COLUMN n TO renamed;\nSELECT renamed FROM t ORDER BY id;'),
+ ('alter-drop-column','ALTER TABLE t DROP COLUMN n;\nSELECT * FROM t ORDER BY id;'),
+ ('upsert','INSERT INTO t(id,v,n) VALUES(1,\'changed\',4) ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET n=excluded.n;\nSELECT n FROM t WHERE id=1;'),
+ ('returning','UPDATE t SET n=n+1 WHERE id=1 RETURNING id,n;'),
+ ('json-type',"SELECT json_type('{\"a\":[1]}','$.a');"),
+ ('json-array-length',"SELECT json_array_length('[1,2,3]');"),
+ ('json-each',"SELECT key,type,value FROM json_each('[1,2,3]') ORDER BY key;"),
+ ('json-tree',"SELECT fullkey,type FROM json_tree('{\"a\":[1]}') ORDER BY id;"),
+ ('json-insert',"SELECT json_insert('{\"a\":1}','$.b',2);"),
+ ('date-function',"SELECT date('2001-02-03','+1 day');"),
+ ('strftime',"SELECT strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S','2001-02-03 04:05:06');"),
+ ('changes','UPDATE t SET n=n+1 WHERE id<=2;\nSELECT changes();'),
+ ('total-changes','UPDATE t SET n=n+1 WHERE id<=2;\nSELECT total_changes();'),
+ ('last-insert-rowid',"INSERT INTO t(v,n) VALUES('extra',4);\nSELECT last_insert_rowid();"),
+ ('sqlite-version','SELECT sqlite_version();'),
+ ('sqlite-source-id','SELECT sqlite_source_id();'),
+ ('pragma-table-valued',"SELECT name,type FROM pragma_table_info('t') ORDER BY cid;"),
+ ('pragma-user-version','PRAGMA user_version=17;\nPRAGMA user_version;'),
+ ('utf16-reading','SELECT id,v,n FROM t ORDER BY id;'),
+ ('savepoint-after-read','SELECT count(*) FROM t;\nSAVEPOINT s;\nUPDATE t SET n=n+1;\nROLLBACK TO s;\nRELEASE s;\nSELECT n FROM t ORDER BY id;'),
+ ]
+ values=[rng.randrange(1,10000) for _ in range(3)];assert len(set(values))==len(values)
+ setup='CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v TEXT,n INTEGER);'+''.join("INSERT INTO t VALUES("+str(i+1)+",'value "+str(rng.randrange(1000000))+"',"+str(n)+");" for i,n in enumerate(values))
+ # Observe all surviving user tables through C, including an ALTER RENAME target.
+ def observe(path):
+  schema=csql(path,'PRAGMA integrity_check;SELECT type,hex(name),hex(tbl_name),hex(sql) FROM sqlite_schema ORDER BY type,name;')
+  names=csql(path,"SELECT hex(name) FROM sqlite_schema WHERE type='table' ORDER BY name;");rows=[]
+  if names['code']==0:
+   for raw in names['stdout'].splitlines():
+    name=bytes.fromhex(raw).decode();quoted='"'+name.replace('"','""')+'"';rows.append(dict(table=name,result=csql(path,'SELECT * FROM '+quoted+' ORDER BY 1;')))
+  return dict(schema=schema,names=names,rows=rows)
+ cases=[]
+ with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='sqleanite-gap-survey-') as tmp:
+  folder=Path(tmp)
+  for i,(feature,sql) in enumerate(probes):
+   base=folder/(str(i)+'-base.db');prefix="PRAGMA encoding='UTF-16le';" if feature=='utf16-reading' else '';made=csql(base,prefix+setup);assert made['code']==0,made
+   for mode in ['--verified-file','--verified-wal-file']:
+    left=folder/(str(i)+mode+'-lean.db');right=folder/(str(i)+mode+'-sqlite.db');shutil.copyfile(base,left);shutil.copyfile(base,right);l,c=run([str(binary),mode,str(left)],sql),csql(right,sql);lo,co=observe(left),observe(right)
+    matched=not l.get('timed_out',False) and not c.get('timed_out',False) and helpers.same(l,c) and lo==co and lo['schema']['code']==0 and lo['schema']['stdout'].startswith('ok\n')
+    cases.append(dict(feature=feature,sql=sql,mode=mode,lean=l,sqlite=c,normalized_lean_error=helpers.error(l),normalized_sqlite_error=helpers.error(c),observation_lean=lo,observation_sqlite=co,matched=matched))
+    a.output.write_text(json.dumps(dict(complete=False,seed=a.seed,implementation_sha256=source,executable_sha256=exe,cases=cases,failures=sum(not c['matched'] for c in cases)),indent=2)+'\n')
+ assert source==helpers.digest() and exe==hashlib.sha256(binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+ report=dict(complete=True,seed=a.seed,setup=setup,comparison=__doc__.strip(),implementation_sha256=source,executable_sha256=exe,harness_sha256=hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest(),helper_sha256=hashlib.sha256((ROOT/'scripts/run-json-position-differential.py').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),sqlite_version=subprocess.check_output([sqlite,'--version'],text=True).strip(),cases=cases,failures=sum(not c['matched'] for c in cases));a.output.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2)+'\n');print(json.dumps(dict(features=len(probes),cases=len(cases),failures=report['failures'])));return bool(report['failures'])
+if __name__=='__main__':raise SystemExit(main())

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Fresh malformed-trigger EOF/token probes in both public file modes.
+
+Names, values and comments vary under preselected malformed templates. Compare
+exact SQL diagnostics after CLI framing removal, result prefixes and unchanged
+C-observed catalog/data/integrity. A timeout is a failure. These malformed-input
+checks do not claim execution of valid triggers, covered negatively by the gap
+survey. The full parser has separate internal unknown-token-after-body controls.
+"""
+import argparse,hashlib,importlib.util,json,os,random,shutil,subprocess,tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('position',ROOT/'scripts/run-json-position-differential.py');helpers=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(helpers)
+def main():
+ p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__);p.add_argument('--seed',type=int,required=True);p.add_argument('--count',type=int,default=64);p.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True);a=p.parse_args();rng=random.Random(a.seed);sqlite=os.environ.get('SQLITE3_BIN','sqlite3');binary=ROOT/'.lake/build/bin/sqleanite';source=helpers.digest();exe=hashlib.sha256(binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+ def run(cmd,sql):
+  try:
+   q=subprocess.run(cmd,input=sql.encode(),capture_output=True,timeout=10);return dict(code=q.returncode,stdout=q.stdout.decode('latin1'),stderr=q.stderr.decode('latin1'))
+  except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as expired:return dict(code=124,stdout=(expired.stdout or b'').decode('latin1'),stderr=(expired.stderr or b'').decode('latin1'),timed_out=True,timeout_seconds=10)
+ def csql(path,sql):return run([sqlite,'-batch','-bail',str(path)],sql)
+ inputs=[]
+ for i in range(a.count):
+  value=rng.randrange(1,10000000);name='truncated_'+str(rng.randrange(100000000));unknown='unexpected_'+str(rng.randrange(100000000))
+  body=rng.choice(['','SELECT '+str(value)+';','UPDATE t SET n='+str(value)+';','DELETE FROM t;','INSERT INTO t VALUES('+str(value)+');',unknown+' token'])
+  sql='CREATE TRIGGER '+name+rng.choice([' AFTER UPDATE ',' BEFORE INSERT ',' AFTER DELETE '])+'ON t BEGIN /* body '+str(value)+' */ '+body
+  inputs.append(dict(sql=sql,value=value))
+ observe='PRAGMA integrity_check;SELECT type,hex(name),hex(tbl_name),hex(sql) FROM sqlite_schema ORDER BY type,name;SELECT n FROM t ORDER BY n;';cases=[]
+ with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix='sqleanite-trigger-eof-') as tmp:
+  folder=Path(tmp)
+  for i,entry in enumerate(inputs):
+   base=folder/(str(i)+'-base.db');made=csql(base,'CREATE TABLE t(n INTEGER);INSERT INTO t VALUES('+str(entry['value'])+');');assert made['code']==0,made
+   for mode in ['--verified-file','--verified-wal-file']:
+    left=folder/(str(i)+mode+'-lean.db');right=folder/(str(i)+mode+'-sqlite.db');shutil.copyfile(base,left);shutil.copyfile(base,right)
+    l,c=run([str(binary),mode,str(left)],entry['sql']),csql(right,entry['sql']);lo,co=csql(left,observe),csql(right,observe)
+    matched=not l.get('timed_out') and not c.get('timed_out') and c['code']!=0 and helpers.same(l,c) and lo==co and lo['code']==0 and lo['stdout'].startswith('ok\n')
+    cases.append(dict(input=i,mode=mode,lean=l,sqlite=c,observation_lean=lo,observation_sqlite=co,matched=bool(matched)))
+ assert source==helpers.digest() and exe==hashlib.sha256(binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
+ report=dict(seed=a.seed,inputs=inputs,cases=cases,failures=sum(not c['matched'] for c in cases),comparison=__doc__.strip(),implementation_sha256=source,executable_sha256=exe,harness_sha256=hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest(),helper_sha256=hashlib.sha256((ROOT/'scripts/run-json-position-differential.py').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),sqlite_version=subprocess.check_output([sqlite,'--version'],text=True).strip());a.output.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2)+'\n');print(json.dumps(dict(cases=len(cases),failures=report['failures'])));return bool(report['failures'])
+if __name__=='__main__':raise SystemExit(main())
